# Phase 88 — Demo-Ready: Root-Cause Closure of the Phase 87 Capstone Gaps

Phase 88 is a **root-cause closure phase**: no new page family, no new navigation zone, no new capability, no change to the `matter`/`client` wire vocabulary or the pinned audit literals ([ADR-342](../adr/ADR-342-audit-and-entity-tag-strings-stable-under-renamed-wire.md)). The Phase 87 capstone (617C, PR #1641) walked the legal-za 90-day story on the real Keycloak stack and found 59 gaps (5 HIGH), 53 failed steps and 5 of 11 exit checkpoints failing; ten research dossiers (`tasks/phase88-research/R1…R10`) traced them to **24 root-cause units** ([register §2](phase88-gap-register.md)). Phase 88 fixes those units at the source in the register's order, with one tenant migration series starting at **V4** (V5+ once V4 has been applied to a persistent stack, §13.7) and one global migration (**V2**), re-walks the scenario twice — mid-phase (Days 1–7, 36–47, 61–75) and at the end (all 90 days) — and exits only on **observed** evidence: zero HIGH, zero FAIL outside the authorised scenario amendments (register §5), E.1/E.2/E.3/E.8/E.9 PASS, legal + default + portal Playwright green on a fresh stack. Architecture: [`architecture/phase88-demo-ready-root-cause-closure.md`](../architecture/phase88-demo-ready-root-cause-closure.md) (Section 13); requirements: [`requirements/claude-code-prompt-phase88.md`](../requirements/claude-code-prompt-phase88.md) (U1–U24); ADRs [345](../adr/ADR-345-canonical-project-role-values.md)–[357](../adr/ADR-357-terminology-guard-extension.md).

**Baseline the closure report measures against (§13.1, re-confirmed by `ls` on 2026-10-23 at HEAD `50b88ed5c`):** 103 `@Entity` classes; 27 `Capability` constants (frontend `CAPABILITIES` lists 10); audit catalogue **29** (`AuditEventTypeRegistryTest` `hasSize(29)` ×3 at `:84,131,132`); tenant migrations V1–V3 (`V3__normalise_work_types.sql`), global V1 (`V1__baseline.sql`); 57 `page.tsx` under `org/[slug]`; 18 portal pages; `matter-tab-groups.ts` 7 groups / **21** tabs; `InvoiceStatus` 5 values; `AcceptanceStatus` 6 values; ≈589 user-visible `Project`/`Customer`/`Invoice` sites (R8 §1); carried Playwright failures **six** (617C C.8); full backend verify ≈ 12.5 min; capstone evidence `qa_cycle/evidence/phase87-capstone/`; findings `tasks/phase87-capstone-findings.md`.

**Migrations: tenant V4 series + global V2 (§13.7).** 619B creates `V4__phase88_demo_ready.sql` (§1 roles); 620A appends §2 (payment terms + due-date backfill); 622B appends §3 (balance model) **only if no persistent stack has applied V4** — otherwise it opens the next free file. Epic 623 (mid-phase re-walk) applies the tenant series to the Keycloak dev DB, so every later migration-bearing slice (624F §6 decline, 626A §4 letterhead, 627B §5 retention, 627H §7 comment attachments) **expects** to open a new file (V5, V6, V7…) — the builder runs `ls $M/tenant | sort -V | tail -1` and reads `flyway_schema_history` on one Keycloak tenant schema before naming the file (memory `feedback_migration_numbers_next_free`). The `§n` numbers below name the architecture's sections (§13.7.1), not the file. Global V2 (`V2__portal_contact_directory.sql`) is owned by 624A alone, after `BaselineSchemaParityTest`'s global branch is re-pinned to target `"1"` (§13.7.3). Every DDL guard scoped to `current_schema()`; every statement idempotent; the two V1 baselines are immutable.

**Epic → architecture slice mapping (founder decision, one-to-one):** E1→618, E2→619, E3→620, E4→621, E5→622, E6→623, E7→624, E8→625, E9→626, E10→627, E11→628, E12→629 (§13.10). **One PR per slice** (Phase 87 practice, PRs #1588–#1641; root `CLAUDE.md` §7 one fix per PR; same-class clusters inside a unit are authorised by the register §2). Branch convention: `phase88/<nn><slice-letter>-<slug>` (e.g. `phase88/02B-v4-role-migration`). Every product slice **starts with a reproduce step** (browser on the :3001 mock stack; the Keycloak stack for the *KC-only* units U19 and U11-prod) and **ends with an observed PASS** naming the register gap ids it closes (root `CLAUDE.md` §3, §4). Real-data integration tests are explicit tasks for U1, U3, U4, U5, U6 (requirements constraint).

**Path shorthands used below:** `$B` = `backend/src/main/java/io/b2mash/b2b/b2bstrawman`, `$T` = `backend/src/test/java/io/b2mash/b2b/b2bstrawman`, `$R` = `backend/src/main/resources`, `$M` = `$R/db/migration`, `$F` = `frontend`, `$FA` = `frontend/app/(app)/org/[slug]`, `$P` = `portal`, `$E` = `frontend/e2e/tests`, `$Q` = `qa/testplan/demos`, `$EV` = `qa_cycle/evidence/phase88-rewalk`. **Gate shorthands:** **BE-VERIFY** = `cd backend && ./mvnw spotless:apply && bash scripts/verify.sh` with no positional goals, run detached with a marker file, one verify at a time (memory `background_bash_timeout_full_verify`); **FE-GATE** = `cd frontend && rm -rf .next && SHELL=/bin/bash NODE_OPTIONS="" pnpm lint && pnpm format:check && pnpm test && pnpm build`; **PORTAL-GATE** = `cd portal && pnpm lint && pnpm format:check && pnpm test && pnpm build`; **E2E-LEGAL** = fresh `bash compose/scripts/e2e-down.sh && bash compose/scripts/e2e-up.sh` (gate-grade; `e2e-reseed.sh` does not wipe — memory `env_playwright_legal_configs_dirty_tree`) then `cd frontend && E2E_AUTH_MODE=mock PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL=http://localhost:3001 pnpm test:e2e:legal-lifecycle` **and** `pnpm test:e2e:legal-depth-ii`, run in a worktree, `git checkout -- frontend/test-results documentation/screenshots` after; **E2E-DEFAULT** = `pnpm test:e2e` (default config) on the same fresh stack; **E2E-PORTAL** = `cd portal && pnpm test:e2e:portal-client-90day` + `responsive` + `frontend/e2e/tests/portal/portal-data-isolation.spec.ts`; **KC-STACK** = `cd backend && ./mvnw -q clean compile -DskipTests`, `docker builder prune -f`, `bash compose/scripts/svc.sh start all` (memory `backend_restart_stale_classes`); **MARKERS** = `.claude/markers/verify-{backend,frontend,portal}.json` per touched area (`.claude/markers/README.md`). **Review:** every PR — docs-only included — gets CodeRabbit (bounded wait; `DEFERRED` only on genuine no-show) plus the two `feature-dev:code-reviewer` passes and the audit-trail block in the PR body; reviewers read the diff and independently re-verify the observed-PASS claim.

Adding `matters/[id]/not-found.tsx` (Option A) is enough, because it catches below the layout boundary. Observed on the :3001 production build (direct load + client nav). **Slice count: 75** (twelve epics; product slices sized to the Builder budget — ≤ 8–12 files, ≤ ~800 new lines, never backend + frontend + portal in one slice; migration section + entity + service + controller + access control + tests always split across ≥ 2 slices; the two U21 literal sweeps exceed the file cap only where every change is a mechanical substitution and say so, as Phase 87's rename slices did).

**Parallel tracks (queue-independent, never built concurrently — memory `feedback_sequential_derisked_execution`):** 618 ∥ 619A–619C (the role fix does not touch `SecurityConfig` or `lib/capabilities.tsx`); 624A–624D ∥ 621/622 after 620 (global V2, the directory, the request-link flow and the portal login do not read fee-note code; 624A's `PortalInvoiceView.amountPaid` mapping is the one touchpoint and it reads 622C's `InvoiceSyncEvent` — if 624A is queued before 622C it leaves the mapping to 624H's backend-free slice and 622C adds it instead; record which); 625 ∥ 620–624 after 619 (no migration; recommended slot after 624 so 625A migrates 624F's literal `project_id` key to `AuditDetails.matterScoped` and `MatterFeedIntegrationTest` covers `acceptance.declined` + payment events); 627A/627D/627E/627F ∥ 626 after 625. Everything else is strictly serial: migration-bearing slices in file order (619B → 620A → 622B → 624F → 626A → 627B → 627H), 625E before 625F/626D/626G/628C (reconcile framework), 628 last among code epics (touches ~130 files; rebases cheaply only when nothing else is open).

Status vocabulary: VERIFIED / PASS / PARTIAL / DEFERRED — no PASS-with-note. TASKS.md status rows are the orchestrator's. |

### Merge-conflict hotspots and PR rules

PR rules restated (non-negotiable):
- One PR per slice; backend, frontend and portal never share a PR; a migration section never shares a PR with the controller/access-control layer of its unit (≥ 2 slices per migration-bearing unit).
- **Reproduce before fix** (root `CLAUDE.md` §4): the PR body carries the reproduction evidence path (screenshot / log / SELECT output), the register unit, the dossier section, and the observed PASS after the fix with the gap ids closed. "Code looks right" is not PASS.
- Any Playwright spec a slice breaks is amended **in the same PR** (D13 / lesson B-7); the strict-assertion rule applies from 628E onward and to every new spec before it.
- Pack JSON edits ship with a `reconcile` block and a seeder reconcile test in the same PR (ADR-356); "fresh tenants only" is a review reject.
- Full BE-VERIFY for any slice touching Java; FE-GATE for `frontend/` or `packages/`; PORTAL-GATE for `portal/` or `packages/`; targeted `-Dtest=` runs are inner-loop only; `compose/seed` and `frontend/e2e` changes carry the frontend marker.
- Never edit `tasks/phase88-gap-register.md` §4 or `qa/testplan/demos/*.md` beyond the §5 list; new scenario mismatches are filed as gaps.

| `$B/invoice/{Invoice,InvoiceTransitionService,InvoiceCreationService}.java` | 620A (approve), 622A (lines), 622B (balance), 622C (sources), 625A (`project_id` details) | serial; rebase before each PR |

## Epic 618: Security Seams — Acceptance PDF `frame-ancestors` + Owner-Only Capabilities Client-Side (U12, U18)

**Goal**: Close the one production-affecting HIGH that needs no data (G-B7: Spring's default `X-Frame-Options: DENY` on the main `@Order(2)` chain refuses the acceptance PDF iframe on both the VPS cross-origin and the AWS same-origin topologies) with a request-matcher-scoped header policy — `Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors <portal origin>` and no XFO on `GET /api/portal/acceptance/*/pdf` only, `DENY` everywhere else on both chains — plus an "Open in new tab" fallback on the portal page; and close G-D7 (an Admin reaches Step 2 of the closure dialog and gets the backend 403) by mirroring `Capability.OWNER_ONLY` in `lib/capabilities.tsx`, making `hasCapability` owner-aware and dropping `caps.isAdmin` from the closure server action, with a drift test that reads `Capability.java`.

**References**: Architecture §13.3.1 (U12), §13.3.2 (U18), §13.4.4, §13.6.1, §13.6.2, §13.8.1 (U12 row), §13.8.2 (U18, U12 rows), §13.8.6, §13.9.4, §13.10 E1, §13.12 A10; [ADR-352](../adr/ADR-352-acceptance-pdf-frame-ancestors.md), [ADR-355](../adr/ADR-355-owner-only-capabilities-client-side.md); R5 Q3, R7 Q6.

**Dependencies**: Phase 87 merged (done). 618 ∥ 619A–619C.

**Scope**: Backend (618A) + Portal (618B) + Frontend (618C) + gate (618D).

**Estimated Effort**: S

**Status**: **Done** — 618A (PR #1643), 618B (PR #1644), 618C (PR #1645), 618D epic gate (no code).

### Architecture Decisions
- **Two `DelegatingRequestMatcherHeaderWriter`s on the existing `@Order(2)` chain, not a third chain; `frame-ancestors` replaces XFO on the PDF path only; malformed portal origin fails startup** ([ADR-352](../adr/ADR-352-acceptance-pdf-frame-ancestors.md), §13.6.1, A10).
- **The backend is the authority; the client mirror exists only to avoid gate flicker and must never be wider than `OrgRoleService.resolveCapabilities`** ([ADR-355](../adr/ADR-355-owner-only-capabilities-client-side.md), §13.6.2).

### Non-scope
- Widening the frontend `CAPABILITIES` role-editor map. Custom firm portal domains (a list in the directive is a config change, not an ADR). Any change to `PortalAcceptanceController`.

## Epic 619: Matter Team — Canonical Project Roles (tenant V4 §1) + Creator Auto-Add + Team Tab + `canManage` Gates (U1 → U2)

**Goal**: Fix the data defect with 17 readers — `ProjectTemplateService` writes `"LEAD"` (two sites) and `LegalDemoDataSeeder` `"CONTRIBUTOR"` while thirteen readers and the partial unique lead index expect `"lead"`/`"member"` and four readers expect `"LEAD"` — by routing every writer and reader through `Roles.PROJECT_LEAD`/`PROJECT_MEMBER`, normalising at the entity (`Roles.normaliseProjectRole`: lower-case, unknown → `member`), and migrating existing rows in tenant **V4 §1** in ADR-345's safe order (map strays → demote duplicate leads case-insensitively keeping the earliest → lower-case → `CHECK`); then restore the membership path the capstone lacked ("Carol can't", eight FAILs + two DEFERREDs): template instantiation auto-adds the creator (lead when none chosen, member otherwise), add/remove/transfer are gated to `ProjectAccess.canManage` (Owner, Admin, current lead — `POST …/members` was ungated, `transferLead` refused Owner/Admin), and a **Team** sub-tab under Details (7 groups / **22** tabs, `members → team` aliased) mounts the existing `ProjectMembersPanel`, add-member and transfer-lead dialogs, with `matters/[id]/not-found.tsx` for non-members. Every projection the capstone found empty (Matters "Fee earner —", lead 403, lead notifications, automation `PROJECT_OWNER`) is asserted by a **real-write-path** integration test that instantiates a template through `ProjectTemplateService`.

**References**: Architecture §13.2.1, §13.3.3 (U1), §13.3.4 (U2), §13.4.1, §13.5.1, §13.7.1 §1, §13.8.1 (U1, U2 rows), §13.8.2 (U2 row), §13.8.4 (`findLeadsByProjectIdIn` constant), §13.8.6, §13.9.1, §13.10 E2, §13.12 #4; [ADR-345](../adr/ADR-345-canonical-project-role-values.md), [ADR-346](../adr/ADR-346-matter-visibility-and-team-management.md); R1 §0–§6; register D1.

**Dependencies**: 618 (hook: V4 file naming agreed; 619A–619C may be queued while 618 is open).

**Scope**: Backend (619A, 619B, 619C) + Frontend (619D) + E2E/gate (619E).

**Estimated Effort**: L

**Status**:

| Slice | Tasks | Files Touched | Summary |
|-------|-------|---------------|---------|
| **619A** | 619.1–619.3 | `Roles`, `ProjectMember`, `ProjectTemplateService` (×2 sites), `LegalDemoDataSeeder`, `NotificationService` (×3), `AssignMemberActionExecutor`, `CreateTaskActionExecutor`, `McpMatterDto` javadoc + 4 test edits + 1 new unit test | Reproduce G-D11 a / G-D13; constants at every writer/reader; entity normaliser; existing pins flipped. **Done** (PR #1646, ef63813cf). Census: every 619.2 line ref correct; no missed writer/reader; `ProjectMemberRepository:40` / `MatterListRowRepository:46` `'lead'` literals and the case-tolerant `ProjectContextBuilder:154` left as-is (already canonical). ### Architecture Decisions
- **One canonical value set, normalised at every writer and at the entity, CHECK-enforced, migrated in the index-safe order (demote before lower-case)** ([ADR-345](../adr/ADR-345-canonical-project-role-values.md), §13.7.1 §1).
- **Members keep membership-only visibility; Owner, Admin or the current lead manage the team; the creator is auto-added on instantiation; Team is a sub-tab under Details** ([ADR-346](../adr/ADR-346-matter-visibility-and-team-management.md), §13.9.1).
- **A test that never drives the real write path proves nothing about the read path** — `TemplateCreatedMatterReadPathIntegrationTest` is the convention guard (§13.1, §13.8.6).

### Non-scope
- An "all staff can read" firm setting (deferred by ADR-346). A `project_member.lead_transferred` audit key. Changing the task assignee picker's source.

## Epic 620: Fee-Note Truth — Payment Terms + Due Date at Approve (V4 §2) + Outstanding Summary + Checklist-First FICA (U3, U4)

**Goal**: Make the debtor book a function of real data. No code path computes `due_date` outside batch billing and no firm payment-terms setting exists, so every surface Phase 87 put the debtor book on (Home Debtors, Clients "Outstanding fee notes", the aging report, the cash digest, the dunning scan, the PDF's "Due Date:") is empty — U3 adds `BillingSettings.defaultPaymentTermsDays` (V4 §2, default 30), stamps `dueDate = issueDate + terms` at **approve** (the moment the number is allocated), backfills APPROVED/SENT rows, and moves the Fee notes page's client-side "Total Outstanding" sum to `GET /api/invoices/outstanding-summary` computed with the debtor-book predicate through one shared `CollectionsSql` constant. `FicaStatusProjector` reads `information_requests` on a pack the onboarding flow never creates, so FICA reads "outstanding" after a completed checklist — U4 reads the client's onboarding checklist instances first (templates with `auto_instantiate = true`, A1/A2), information-request rule as fallback, `checklistInstanceId` on the response, in two batched queries. Both units carry **real-write-path** tests (approve → send into the debtor book; checklist completion through `TestChecklistHelper`).

**References**: Architecture §13.2.2, §13.3.5 (U3), §13.3.6 (U4), §13.4.3 (`outstanding-summary`, `/api/settings/billing`), §13.5.2, §13.7.1 §2, §13.8.1 (U3, U4 rows), §13.8.2 (U3, U4 rows), §13.8.4 (`findIdsByAutoInstantiateTrue`, `CollectionsSql`), §13.8.6, §13.9.3, §13.10 E3, §13.12 #5, A1–A2; [ADR-347](../adr/ADR-347-fee-note-due-date-and-balance-model.md), [ADR-348](../adr/ADR-348-fica-status-source-of-truth.md); R2 §1–§3; register D2.

**Dependencies**: 619 (V4 file exists).

**Scope**: Backend (620A, 620B, 620D) + Frontend (620C, 620E).

**Estimated Effort**: M

**Status**:

### Architecture Decisions
- **Due date stamped at approve from firm payment terms; backfill `issue_date + terms`; one summary endpoint with the debtor-book definition so no two surfaces can disagree** ([ADR-347](../adr/ADR-347-fee-note-due-date-and-balance-model.md), §13.3.5).
- **Checklist instances first (auto-instantiate templates), information requests as fallback, no persisted column; IN_PROGRESS outranks COMPLETED** ([ADR-348](../adr/ADR-348-fica-status-source-of-truth.md), A1, A2).
- **The five debtor-book sites share one predicate constant guarded by a source scan** (§13.8.4, OQ #12).

### Non-scope
- `PARTIALLY_PAID` and `amount_paid` (622). A persisted FICA column. Raising the list-row statement caps.

## Epic 621: Disbursements — Write-Off + Restore UI, PAYMENT Trust Link, Unbilled Strip (U14)

**Goal**: Close the Day-45 write-off chain and the Day-5 trust-linked disbursement chain: the write-off backend (endpoint, DTO, `WRITE_OFF_DISBURSEMENTS`, audit, tests) is complete but `writeOffDisbursement` has zero callers and `LegalDisbursement.restore()` has no endpoint (G-C9, N-8); the trust-link dialog filters `DISBURSEMENT_PAYMENT`, which is not a value of the ledger CHECK while the service expects `PAYMENT` (G-B11); the unbilled summary endpoint exists and is consumed only by the invoice editor (G-B12). Add a tiny backend (`POST …/{id}/restore`, honoured `matterId/type/status/excludeLinked` filters on the matter-scoped trust transaction read), the write-off dialog + Undo action on the detail page, firm list and matter tab (offered for any UNBILLED row, A14), the PAYMENT filter that hides already-linked payments, and an "Unbilled disbursements R 2 000,00 · VAT R 300,00" strip.

**References**: Architecture §13.3.7, §13.4.2, §13.8.1 (U14 row), §13.8.2 (U14 row), §13.9.2, §13.10 E4, A14; R6 §1–§3; register items 19–21.

**Dependencies**: 620.

**Scope**: Backend (621A) + Frontend (621B, 621C) + E2E/gate (621D).

**Estimated Effort**: M

**Status**: In progress — 621A **Done** (PR #1657), 621B **Done** (PR #1658), 621C **Done** (PR #1659)

**Create (backend):** none (endpoint on the existing controller). **Create (frontend):** `$F/components/legal/{disbursement-write-off-dialog,unbilled-disbursements-summary}.tsx` + tests.
**Modify (backend):** `$B/verticals/legal/disbursement/{DisbursementController,DisbursementService}.java`, `$B/audit/AuditEventTypeRegistry.java`, `$B/verticals/legal/trustaccounting/transaction/{TrustTransactionController,TrustTransactionService,TrustTransactionRepository}.java`, their tests, `$T/audit/AuditEventTypeRegistryTest.java`.
**Modify (frontend):** `$FA/legal/disbursements/{actions.ts,disbursements-list-client.tsx}`, `$FA/legal/disbursements/[id]/detail-client.tsx`, `$F/components/legal/{disbursement-list-view,project-disbursements-tab,trust-transaction-link-dialog}.tsx`, `$F/lib/schemas/legal.ts`, `$F/lib/api/legal-disbursements.ts`, the two depth-ii specs.
**Read for context:** `$B/verticals/legal/disbursement/LegalDisbursement.java` (`restore()`), `$B/verticals/legal/disbursement/DisbursementUnbilledEndpointIntegrationTest`-covered endpoint, `$F/components/legal/disbursement-approval-panel.tsx`, `$FA/legal/disbursements/actions.ts:285-289`.

### Architecture Decisions
- **Mirror the service rule in the UI (write-off for any UNBILLED row); hide already-linked payments so double-linking is impossible** (§13.3.7, A14).
- **The ledger is right (`PAYMENT`); the dialog was wrong — no `DISBURSEMENT_PAYMENT` type is introduced** (§13.1 Out of scope).

### Non-scope
- A `DISBURSEMENT_PAYMENT` trust type. Fee-note line changes (622). Disbursement reversal semantics.

## Epic 622: Fee-Note Lines and Payments — Narrative Lines, Balance Model + `PARTIALLY_PAID` (V4 §3 or V5), Every Source Posts Its Amount, Pre-Check Copy (U15)

**Goal**: Make a fee note bill the attorney's narrative and carry a balance. `buildTimeEntryDescription` emits `taskTitle -- date -- member` and never reads `time_entries.description` (G-C10) → `"{yyyy-MM-dd} · {Fee earner} · {narrative}"` with the task title as fallback, carried into preview, PDF and email. `RecordPaymentRequest` carries only a reference, the amount is always `invoice.total`, and the trust fee-transfer, pay-now webhook and Xero paths all mark PAID regardless of amount (G-C11, N-5) → founder-decided **full part payments** (D8): `PaymentMethod` enum, per-payment `amount`, `invoices.amount_paid` + derived `balanceDue`, `InvoiceStatus.PARTIALLY_PAID`, one internal `InvoiceTransitionService.postPayment` for every source (manual overpayment 409; CARD/XERO/TRUST_TRANSFER capped at PAID + `invoice.payment_excess`), reversal stays internal and now accepts PARTIALLY_PAID; every debtor-book read switches to `status IN ('SENT','PARTIALLY_PAID')` / `total − amount_paid` through the `CollectionsSql` constants. The tax-number pre-check message becomes severity-aware on both sides (G-C6).

**References**: Architecture §13.2.3, §13.2.4, §13.3.8, §13.4.3, §13.4.7 (`RecordPaymentRequest` break; `InvoiceStatus` consumers grep), §13.5.2, §13.5.4, §13.7.1 §3, §13.8.1 (U15 row), §13.8.2 (U15 row), §13.8.4, §13.8.6, §13.9.3, §13.10 E5, A3–A5; [ADR-347](../adr/ADR-347-fee-note-due-date-and-balance-model.md); R6 §4–§6; register D8.

**Dependencies**: 620 (terms, `CollectionsSql`), 621 (write-off before the Day-75 closure re-walk).

**Scope**: Backend (622A, 622B, 622C, 622D) + Frontend (622E) + E2E/gate (622F).

**Estimated Effort**: L

**Status**: **Done** — 622A (PR #1661), 622B (PR #1662), 622C (PR #1663), 622D (PR #1664), 622E (PR #1665), 622F epic gate (PR #1666).

### Key Files

**Create (backend):** `$B/invoice/PaymentMethod.java`, `$B/event/InvoicePartiallyPaidEvent.java`, `$T/invoice/{InvoiceLineDescriptionIntegrationTest,V4InvoiceStatusCheckTest}.java`.
**Modify (backend):** `$M/tenant/V4__phase88_demo_ready.sql` (§3) or NEW `V5__phase88_payment_balance.sql`, `$B/invoice/{InvoiceStatus,Invoice,PaymentEvent,InvoiceTransitionService,InvoiceCreationService,InvoiceValidationService,PaymentReconciliationService}.java`, `$B/invoice/dto/{RecordPaymentRequest,InvoiceResponse}.java`, `$B/audit/AuditEventTypeRegistry.java`, `$B/verticals/legal/trustaccounting/transaction/TrustTransactionService.java`, `$B/integration/accounting/sync/AccountingSyncService.java`, `$B/customerbackend/event/InvoiceSyncEvent.java`, `$B/collections/{CollectionsSql,CollectionsReadService,CashDigestService,CollectionsScanService}.java`, `$B/project/MatterListRowRepository.java`, `$B/customer/{ClientListRowRepository,CustomerService}.java`, `$B/reporting/InvoiceAgingReportQuery.java`, the named tests.
**Modify (frontend):** `$F/components/invoices/{invoice-status-sections,status-badge,PaymentEventHistory,XeroStatusChip,invoice-generation-dialog}.tsx`, `$FA/invoices/invoice-payment-actions.ts`, `$FA/invoices/collections/*`, `$F/lib/types/invoice.ts`, `$F/components/dashboard/debtors-widget.tsx`, tests, `$E/invoices/invoice-lifecycle.spec.ts`.
**Read for context:** `InvoiceTransitionService:330-333` (today's PAID path), `TrustTransactionService:1140`, `PaymentEventRepository`, `customerbackend/handler/PortalEventHandler.java`, R6 §4–§6.

### Architecture Decisions
- **Persisted `amount_paid`, derived `balanceDue`, `PARTIALLY_PAID`; one internal entry point for every payment source; manual overpayment refused, external sources capped at PAID with `invoice.payment_excess`; reversal internal only** ([ADR-347](../adr/ADR-347-fee-note-due-date-and-balance-model.md), A3–A5).
- **Line description is the attorney's narrative (date · fee earner · narrative), task title only as fallback** (§13.3.8).
- **`RecordPaymentRequest` contract break is deliberate and single-client** (§13.4.7).

### Non-scope
- Credit notes. A payment-reversal REST endpoint. Voiding a PARTIALLY_PAID fee note. The portal fee-note balance (624H) and `portal_invoices.amount_paid` (global V2, 624A).

## Epic 623: Mid-Phase Re-Walk — Days 1–7, 36–47, 61–75 on Keycloak (gate epic, no product code)

**Goal**: Observe, on the real Keycloak stack with Playwright MCP in the browser, Mailpit for mail and read-only SELECTs for state, that the three HIGH chains the first five epics targeted now pass: Days 1–7 (G-B3 "Carol can't" + G-D11/G-D13 fee earner), Days 36–47 (G-C9 write-off, G-C10 narrative lines, G-C11 part payments, G-D21 debtors), Days 61–75 (closure gates with write-offs and balances); re-shoot the deck screenshots that showed "No client owes fees." / "Fee earner —"; write the evidence under `$EV/mid/`; file anything new as a gap for its owning epic — **no fixes in this epic**. This walk is also the first persistent application of tenant V4, which fixes the migration numbering for every later epic (OQ #9).

## Epic 624: Portal — Contact Directory (global V2) + Email-Only Sign-In + Link Builder Sweep + Client Notification Intent + Decline (U11 → U13, U23)

**Goal**: Let a client sign in from any email and let every client-facing link land. `portal_contacts` is per-tenant with no global email index, `POST /portal/auth/request-link` requires `orgId`, the bare `/login` short-circuits, and nine of eleven email builders emit bare paths (G-D1, the 68.x DEFERREDs) → global **V2** creates `portal.portal_contact_directory` (`(email_lower, org_id)` presence rows, no tenant secrets) maintained from the tenant-side contact lifecycle after commit and backfilled by an `ApplicationRunner` with a ShedLock lease (A8, A9); the request accepts email alone and sends one branded link per resolved firm with the same generic response for 0/1/N; one `PortalLinkBuilder` is used by all eleven builders and an ArchUnit rule forbids `portalBaseUrl` concatenation elsewhere; the portal forwards `orgId` from the incoming URL. The SoA and closure letter are born PORTAL and emailed on generation with no UI (G-C5, G-D14 emails) → `shareWithClient` / `notifyClient` intent flags (default off), INTERNAL until shared, a "Share with client" action, the allowlist removed (D7); the portal matter card renders `status` (G-D19). Decline (G-B19) is **built**: `DECLINED` + columns (§6, new file V5 per OQ #9), portal decline endpoint, firm notification, read-model sync, badge; the active-unique index frees the pair so re-send works (A12). The portal fee-note view shows the balance (`portal_invoices.amount_paid`, global V2 — OQ #13).

**References**: Architecture §13.2.7, §13.2.9, §13.2.11, §13.3.9 (U11), §13.3.16 (U13), §13.3.21 (U23), §13.4.4, §13.4.5, §13.5.3, §13.5.5, §13.6.3, §13.6.6, §13.7.1 §6, §13.7.2, §13.7.3, §13.8.1 (U11, U13, U23 rows), §13.8.2 (U11, U13, U23 rows), §13.8.6, §13.9.4, §13.10 E7, A8, A9, A12, A15; [ADR-350](../adr/ADR-350-client-emails-explicit-intent.md), [ADR-351](../adr/ADR-351-portal-contact-directory.md), [ADR-349](../adr/ADR-349-activity-feed-correlation-key.md) (`acceptance.declined` carries `project_id`); R5 Q1, Q2, Q4–Q6; register D6, D7.

**Dependencies**: 622 (code: `InvoiceSyncEvent.amountPaid`), 623 (V-number decision for §6). 624A–624D may be queued after 620 (see Parallel tracks).

**Scope**: Backend (624A, 624B, 624C, 624E, 624F) + Portal (624D, 624H) + Frontend (624G) + gate (624I).

**Estimated Effort**: L

**Status**:

### Architecture Decisions
- **Global `(email_lower, org_id)` presence directory; N firms → N branded links; generic response; tokens minted inside the target tenant; application-side backfill with a marker table** ([ADR-351](../adr/ADR-351-portal-contact-directory.md), §13.6.3, A8, A9).
- **Every client-facing link goes through `PortalLinkBuilder`; the ArchUnit rule is how nine-of-eleven drift cannot recur** (§13.6.6).
- **Client emails only on explicit intent; generated documents INTERNAL until shared; the allowlist is removed and its column left in place** ([ADR-350](../adr/ADR-350-client-emails-explicit-intent.md), A15).
- **DECLINED is terminal for the closure gate; the active-unique index frees the pair so re-send works** (A12, §13.2.7).

### Non-scope
- Dropping `portal_settings.portal_notification_doc_types` (later cleanup migration). A portal account model (ADR-T005 stands). Intra-firm contact choice at login (stays inside the tenant).

---
## Epic 625: Feed, Audit, Automation + Pack-Reconcile Framework — Publisher Obligation, Legal Formatter, Facet Filters, Client Activity, "Document uploaded" (U5, U6, U7)

**Goal**: Make the matter feed and the audit trail readable and complete. `AuditEventRepository.findByProjectId` matches only `details->>'project_id'`, which six publisher families (acceptance, trust recorded/approved/rejected/reversed, fee note created/approved/voided) never write; ~40 reachable keys hit the "performed x on y" fallback; Home has a second formatter; `matter_closure.closed` omits the justification (G-B8, G-D9, G-D10 feed, G-D8) → `project_id` (and `customer_id`) become a **publisher obligation** through `AuditDetails.matterScoped`, the feed query gains the `entity_type = 'project'` branch to backfill history, one `ActivityMessageFormatter` with legal templates and a legal generic default, Home delegates. The audit trail filters are free text against storage keys, the Entity column prints raw literals, the registry says "Invoice", and the client Activity tab cannot see checklist events (G-D6, G-B2, G-D10 labels, E.9) → facet-fed Selects, a 68-literal legal label map, "Fee note" + legal `titleCase`, a three-branch `GET /api/clients/{id}/activity`. The `document-review-notification` rule fires "Document accepted" on upload (G-B13, D4) → renamed "Document uploaded — notify matter lead", seeded disabled. Because pack JSON seeds once per tenant, this epic **builds the reconcile framework** (version gate on `AbstractPackSeeder.reconcileExistingPack`, `reconcile`-block parser, `pack_install` rows for all five pack types) with U7 as the first consumer — 626 and 628 depend on it (ADR-356).

**References**: Architecture §13.3.10 (U5), §13.3.11 (U6), §13.3.12 (U7), §13.4.6 (facets, client activity), §13.6.5 (reconcile mechanism + table), §13.8.1 (U5, U6, U7 rows), §13.8.2 (U6 row), §13.8.4 (`findByProjectId` native), §13.8.5 (formatter pattern), §13.8.6, §13.9.4, §13.10 E8, §13.12 #3; [ADR-349](../adr/ADR-349-activity-feed-correlation-key.md), [ADR-356](../adr/ADR-356-pack-content-reconciliation.md), [ADR-342](../adr/ADR-342-audit-and-entity-tag-strings-stable-under-renamed-wire.md); R3 Q1–Q6, §2 (key table), §4 (label map), `R3-emitted-event-keys.txt`; register D3, D4, D12.

**Dependencies**: 619 (the role fix explains G-B13's double recipient). Recommended slot: after 624 (625A migrates 624F's literal key; `MatterFeedIntegrationTest` covers `acceptance.declined` and the 622 payment keys).

**Scope**: Backend (625A, 625B, 625C, 625E, 625F) + Frontend (625D) + gate (625G).

**Estimated Effort**: L

**Status**: **Done** — 625A (PR #1679), 625B (PR #1680), 625C (PR #1681), 625D (PR #1682), 625E (PR #1683), 625F (PR #1684), 625G epic gate (no code).

**Create (backend):** `$B/audit/AuditDetails.java`, `$B/customer/ClientActivityService.java`, `$B/seeder/PackReconcileEntry.java`, `$T/activity/MatterFeedIntegrationTest.java`, `$T/audit/MatterScopedPublisherGuardTest.java`, `$T/customer/ClientActivityIntegrationTest.java`, `$T/seeder/AbstractPackSeederReconcileTest.java`.

**Modify (frontend):** `$FA/settings/firm/_tabs/{audit-log-client.tsx,audit-trail-actions.ts}`, `$F/components/audit/entity-cell.tsx`, `$FA/clients/[id]/audit-tab.tsx`, `$F/lib/api/{audit-events,clients}.ts`, `$F/__tests__/audit/audit-trail.test.tsx`.

### Architecture Decisions
- **`project_id` in audit details is a publisher obligation guarded by a source scan; the feed query backfills history through the entity branch; one formatter, Home delegates; `justification` is copied, not amended** ([ADR-349](../adr/ADR-349-activity-feed-correlation-key.md), D3).
- **Legal words live in the UI label layer and the registry's display labels only; stored keys and entity literals never change** ([ADR-342](../adr/ADR-342-audit-and-entity-tag-strings-stable-under-renamed-wire.md), D12).
- **Pack content reaches existing tenants through a version-gated, idempotent `from → to` reconcile that never overwrites user edits; built once here, consumed by 625F/626/628** ([ADR-356](../adr/ADR-356-pack-content-reconciliation.md), §13.6.5).
- **"Upload is treated as acceptance" is renamed and disabled, not re-engineered** (D4).

### Non-scope
- A real document-acceptance automation trigger. Rewriting historical audit rows. Facet endpoints (already exist). The U21 label census beyond `"Fee note"` and the legal `titleCase` (628D).

## Epic 626: Documents and Rendering — Letterhead (V6 §4) + Brand CSS + Pack Reconcile, Thymeleaf Preview Retired, Closure Arithmetic, OTP Clauses, Preview Wrapper, "Not saved" (U8 → U9 → U10)

**Goal**: Make the documents a design partner sees carry the firm. `OrgSettings` has no address, phone, email, bank or signatory field, so `org.bankingDetails` and `org.principal_attorney` are dangling template keys; `document-default.css` references `var(--brand-color)` that nothing defines and OpenHTMLToPDF has no CSS variables; the engagement letters never reference `org.logoUrl`; the fee note prints "Banking details are available on request" (G-B5, G-C7 a–c, wow moment 6) → a `LetterheadSettings` embeddable (16 nullable columns, **V6 §4**), `PATCH /api/settings/letterhead`, a form under Firm › General, `buildOrgContext` keys, brand colour injected as concrete CSS rules (validated hex), and the legal-za pack's engagement letters + fee note reconciled onto existing tenants by body hash. The draft Preview is a second Thymeleaf renderer with raw numbers and no logo; tariff dialogs reimplement `R ${toFixed(2)}`; `TrustBalanceZeroGate` prints `"R%s"`; the SoA serialises `DisbursementCategory` raw; the closure letter double-counts disbursements (G-C7 d/e, G-D14 amounts, N-4, G-C12) → the Thymeleaf preview is retired for the Tiptap fee-note template (A6), `formatCurrency` everywhere, `DisbursementCategory.label()`, correct closure totals with hint maps. Generation polish (G-B6, G-B16, G-B18, G-C4): "Not saved" rows hidden by default, the acceptance subject names the template, the conveyancing clause pack declares the OTP associations (+ reconcile), and the A4 preview wrapper reads `contentRect.width` after the SoA-only loop is reproduced (OQ #1).

**References**: Architecture §13.2.5, §13.3.13 (U8), §13.3.14 (U9), §13.3.15 (U10), §13.4.6 (`/api/settings/letterhead`), §13.6.4, §13.6.5 (template + clause rows), §13.7.1 §4, §13.8.1 (U8, U9, U10 rows), §13.8.2 (U8, U9, U10 rows), §13.8.3 (embeddable pattern), §13.8.5, §13.8.6, §13.9.4, §13.10 E9, §13.12 #1, A6; [ADR-353](../adr/ADR-353-firm-letterhead-settings.md), [ADR-356](../adr/ADR-356-pack-content-reconciliation.md); R4 §1–§8; register D5.

**Dependencies**: 624 (migration ordering), **625** (reconcile framework).

**Scope**: Backend (626A, 626B, 626D, 626E, 626G) + Frontend (626C, 626F) + E2E/gate (626H).

**Estimated Effort**: L

**Status**: **Done** — 626A **Done** (PR #1685), 626B **Done** (PR #1686), 626C **Done** (PR #1687), 626D **Done** (PR #1688), 626E **Done** (PR #1689), 626F **Done** (PR #1690), 626G **Done** (PR #1691), 626H **Done** (PR #1692; Epic 626 gate).

### Architecture Decisions
- **Letterhead as an embeddable on `OrgSettings`; brand colour injected as validated concrete CSS rules (no CSS variables in OpenHTMLToPDF); packs reference the keys and reconcile by body hash** ([ADR-353](../adr/ADR-353-firm-letterhead-settings.md), §13.6.4).
- **Retire the Thymeleaf draft preview — one renderer, one formatter** (A6, memory `feedback_tiptap_not_thymeleaf`).
- **Money has one source on each side (`VariableFormatter.formatCurrency`, `@b2mash/shared` `formatCurrency`), guarded by a remnant rule** (§13.8.5).
- **Pack text reaches existing tenants through [ADR-356](../adr/ADR-356-pack-content-reconciliation.md); edited templates and clones are never overwritten.**

### Non-scope
- A pack-declared header-field flag. Table-header brand colouring. Changing generated-document persistence. A new preview endpoint path.

## Epic 627: Matter Detail, Retention (V7 §5), Exports (BFF), Small Fixes, Comment Attachments (V7 §7) (U16, U17, U19, U20, U24)

**Goal**: Close the matter-detail and closure-side MEDIUMs and build the last founder-decided feature. "Promotion" means structural columns, so `case_number`/`court_name`/`opposing_party` never reach the header (G-B9); `matter-tabs.tsx` resolves `urlTab ?? userTab` so a click never wins over the URL (G-B14); the New Matter dialog's conflict card is an Epic 371A "Coming Soon" stub (G-A6); the header guards Close Matter with `=== "ACTIVE"` while the backend allows COMPLETED (G-D2) → a `HEADER_FIELD_SLUGS` facts strip (D9), URL-as-source tabs ported from the client tabs, a "Run a conflict check first →" link-out, the guard dropped, and the dead `MatterSidebar`/`MatterDetailLayout` family deleted (A17). `legal_matter_retention_years` is NULL (insert bypasses `DEFAULT 5`), has no UI, the end date is computed into the closure response only and `complete()` starts the clock (G-D3) → seeded 5, editable under Firm › Compliance, `projects.retention_ends_on` (**V7 §5**) stamped at close, reset on re-close, cleared on reopen (D10). `getAuthToken()` throws by design in BFF mode and the `NEXT_REDIRECT` is swallowed (G-D4, N-2, *KC-only*) → exports on the `exportAuditPdf` template + a bespoke profitability CSV endpoint (A7). Four small fixes (G-C2, G-D5, G-D12, G-C1) with OQ #2 deciding whether a backend invitation reconcile ships. Comment attachments (G-B15) are **built**: `comment_attachments` (**V7 §7**), presign-flow upload with the comment's scope and visibility, chips in the firm app and on SHARED comments in the portal (A13).

**References**: Architecture §13.2.6, §13.2.8, §13.3.17 (U16), §13.3.18 (U17), §13.3.19 (U19), §13.3.20 (U20), §13.3.22 (U24), §13.4.5, §13.4.6, §13.7.1 §5, §7, §13.8.1 (U17, U19, U20, U24 rows), §13.8.2 (U16, U17, U19, U20, U24 rows), §13.8.6, §13.9.4, §13.10 E10, §13.12 #2, A7, A11, A13, A17; [ADR-354](../adr/ADR-354-persisted-retention-end-date.md), ADR-249; R7 Q1–Q5, R9 §1–§6; register D9, D10, items 25–34, 36.

**Dependencies**: 626 (migration ordering). 627A / 627D / 627E / 627F may be queued after 625.

**Scope**: Frontend (627A, 627C, 627E, 627F, 627I) + Backend (627B, 627D, 627G conditional, 627H) + Portal (627J) + gate (627K).

**Estimated Effort**: L

**Status**: In progress — 627A **Done** (PR #1693), 627B **Done** (PR #1694), 627C **Done** (PR #1695), 627D **Done** (PR #1696), 627E **Done** (PR #1697), 627F **Done** (PR #1698), 627G **N/A** (OQ #2 ACCEPTED, no PR).

**Read for context:** `$F/lib/constants/promoted-field-slugs.ts`, `$F/components/customers/customer-grouped-tabs.tsx`, `$B/project/Project.java:253-265`, `$B/retention/*` (policy readers stay), `$F/lib/auth/keycloak-bff.ts`, `$B/diary/DiaryController.java:30-45`, `$B/invitation/InvitationController.java:39-44`, `$B/document/DocumentService` (presign), R7, R9.

### Architecture Decisions
- **Hard-coded legal-za header-field list; conflict card links out; Close offered on COMPLETED; the dead sidebar family is deleted** (D9, A17, §13.3.17).
- **Per-matter persisted `retention_ends_on` stamped at close, reset on re-close, cleared on reopen; completion no longer starts the clock; no read-time fallback; firm-wide `retention_policies` row no longer seeded at closure** ([ADR-354](../adr/ADR-354-persisted-retention-end-date.md), A11).
- **BFF-safe exports on the `exportAuditPdf` template; the bespoke profitability page gets its own CSV endpoint** (A7).
- **Comment attachments are a child table, ≤ 5, visibility inherited, `Source.COMMENT`; documents stay first-class** (A13).

### Non-scope
- A pack-declared header-field flag. A backend-validated `GOVERNMENT` party type. Retiring `retention_policies`. The profitability cost leg (Phase 87 §12.14 #1). Inline conflict check with an opposing-party input.

## Epic 628: Terminology Census + Shell Layout + Guard Extension, Playwright Suites + Seed, Scenario-Amendments Docs PR (U21 → U22)

**Goal**: Make the firm app say Matter / Client / Fee note everywhere a user can read, and make the suites prove it. R8's census found ≈589 user-visible legacy sites (261 frontend/portal in 129 files, 24 pack JSON + email HTML, 26 backend labels/registries, 278 backend messages: 4 `ResourceNotFoundException` strings × 132 sites + 146 others), an incomplete breadcrumb map with no entity name in the last crumb, settings tabs "Tariffs"/"Xero" that mislabel their content, a shell column and `<main>` lacking `min-w-0` (the cause 616A/615C/615D worked around), a single-line diary row in ~150 px columns, the org name in the brand colour on a dark rail, tier remnants in `IntegrationCard`, a dead `en-ZA-accounting` file and a dead "Court Calendar" module entry (G-A2/A3/A4/A5/A7, B1, B4, C3, C12, D10, D15–D18, D20, E.8) → the census is rewritten to zero in four source-kind slices, packs and report definitions reconcile onto existing tenants (625E framework), and the Epic 611 remnant guard is extended to JSX/prop literals, pack JSON and backend message strings with one allow-list per side (ADR-357). The Playwright carried six and the default-config/portal failures are locator/flow/seed drift defeated by the strict-mode `isVisible().catch(() => false)` pattern (R10) → suite-wide strict assertions with a lint rule, real-flow specs, seed rows (two invoices, a trust account, the three portal rows), path-anchored per-suite scripts (D13). The register §5 scenario amendments land in one docs PR in the LZKC-029 style before the end-of-phase re-walk.

**References**: Architecture §13.3.23 (U21), §13.3.24 (U22), §13.6.5 (field/report/request rows), §13.8.1 (U21 row), §13.8.2 (U21, U22 rows), §13.8.6, §13.10 E11, A16; [ADR-357](../adr/ADR-357-terminology-guard-extension.md), [ADR-356](../adr/ADR-356-pack-content-reconciliation.md), [ADR-341](../adr/ADR-341-settings-taxonomy-five-families-controls-stay-pages-go.md) (ids kept), [ADR-344](../adr/ADR-344-qa-scenario-amendment-authorisation-phase87.md); R8 §0–§8 (`table-frontend`, `table-backend`, §3 segments, §4 settings nav, §8 test pins), R10 §0, §A–§D; register §5, D11–D13, N-7.

**Dependencies**: 627 (last code units — rebases cheaply only when nothing else is open), **625** (reconcile framework).

**Scope**: Frontend + packages (628A, 628B) + Portal (628B) + Backend (628C, 628D) + E2E (628E, 628F, 628G) + Docs (628H) + gate (628I).

**Estimated Effort**: L

**Status**:

### Architecture Decisions
- **The census is a worklist, not a sample; the guard on both sides holds it at zero with one narrow allow-list per side** ([ADR-357](../adr/ADR-357-terminology-guard-extension.md)).
- **Tab ids stay for redirects while labels change; h1s follow the nav words** (D11, [ADR-341](../adr/ADR-341-settings-taxonomy-five-families-controls-stay-pages-go.md)).
- **Pack and report text reach existing tenants only through [ADR-356](../adr/ADR-356-pack-content-reconciliation.md).**
- **One shell fix (`min-w-0`) retires three page-local workarounds** (§13.3.23, G-D15 determination).
- **Strict assertions suite-wide; seed additions go into the e2e seed, not the specs; Playwright amendments authorised in-slice** (D13, R10).
- **Tier/plan Badge variants retired** (A16, memory `project_no_plan_subscriptions`).

### Non-scope
- Any wire or stored-literal rename. The `/legal/*` route prefix. Rewriting legitimate English prose beyond the census. Scenario amendments outside register §5.

## Epic 629: End-of-Phase Re-Walk (all 90 days, Keycloak) + Closure Report + Verify-Marker Audit (gate epic, no product code)

**Goal**: Prove the exit, not infer it. Walk `legal-za-90day-keycloak.md` (as amended by 628H) end to end on the real Keycloak stack — browser only, Playwright MCP, Mailpit for mail, read-only SELECTs for state, no substitutions — plus the portal touchpoints and `portal-client-90day-keycloak.md`; score every step, the eleven exit checkpoints and the seven wow moments; run the three Playwright suites on a fresh stack; audit the verify markers of every Phase 88 PR; write the gap register and the closure report mirroring Phase 87's C.1–C.13 with evidence paths for every number. Exit: **0 HIGH, 0 FAIL outside the register §5 amendments, E.1/E.2/E.3/E.8/E.9 PASS, legal + default + portal Playwright green on a fresh stack, a marker for every PR**. Anything short of that is reported as PARTIAL with the gaps routed to a follow-up — never narrated around.

**References**: Architecture §13.1 (exit statement), §13.8.6 (Keycloak re-walks row), §13.10 E12, requirements "Mid-phase checkpoint and end-of-phase re-walk"; Phase 87 617C (`tasks/phase87-legal-native-shell.md` § Closure checklist 1–12 and § Closure report C.1–C.13; `tasks/phase87-capstone-findings.md` §2 scorecard, §5 gap register, §7 automated tests); root `CLAUDE.md` §3, §8, §9; `.claude/markers/README.md`.

**Dependencies**: 628 merged (every code epic on `main`).

**Scope**: QA / Process / Docs (docs-only PR).

**Estimated Effort**: M

**Create (docs):** `$EV/full/{README,session-0,day-00,days-01-07,days-08-21,days-22-35,days-36-60,days-61-75,days-76-90,portal-90day,exit-checkpoints,e2e-runs,markers-audit,gaps}.md`, `marketing/decks/screens/phase88/wow-*.png`.
**Modify (docs):** this file (§ Closure report, status rows), `architecture/ARCHITECTURE.md` (one note line), `marketing/decks/exec-deck.html`, `tasks/lessons.md`.
**Read for context:** `$Q/legal-za-90day-keycloak.md` (as amended by 628H), `$Q/portal-client-90day-keycloak.md`, `$Q/legal-za-full-lifecycle-keycloak.md` (context-swap protocol), `qa_cycle/evidence/phase87-capstone/*`, `tasks/phase87-capstone-findings.md`, `$EV/mid/*`.

### Architecture Decisions
- **PASS means observed; the closure report is a draft until its numbers are independently re-run** (root `CLAUDE.md` §3, §8).
- **The re-walk writes gaps, never fixes; new product gaps are a follow-up phase or an authorised amendment, never an in-epic patch** (§13.1).

### Non-scope
- The AI-core walk (needs a funded key — DEFERRED with reason, as in 617C). Phase 84 take-on. Any code.
